# Runenwerk Repository File Census

This is a mechanical appendix for issue #1153. It inventories every tracked blob at accepted Runenwerk main:

```text
34ad02d9b8fac925936ac0d5971abe4d293f1749
```

The semantic architecture decisions live in the companion repository architecture audit. This appendix does not infer architecture from physical placement or file size.

Classification rules:

- `artifact class` is mechanical from path/extension;
- `owner/package` is the longest matching root-workspace member, otherwise the top-level repository area;
- `status` defaults to `current` with explicit mechanical overrides for accepted ADRs, point-in-time investigation reports, the separately owned `domain/ui/**` program, known top-level networking-diagram placement reconciliation, and tracked IDE residue.

Exact tracked blobs: **2461**.
